D84Write/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-connorott-plugins-dev-team-workspace-iteration-b1-minilab-A0-r3-with-skill-project/4cdbaf59-1a50-4935-9c02-64792440706b/scratchpad/samples.py (25 lines)
wrote /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-connorott-plugins-dev-team-workspace-iteration-b1-minilab-A0-r3-with-skill-project/4cdbaf59-1a50-4935-9c02-64792440706b/scratchpad/samples.py
import json
import math
import statistics as st

d = ["2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-06", "2024-03-07", "2024-03-08"]
c = {"AAA": [100.0, 102.0, 101.0, 99.0, 100.5, 103.0], "BBB": [50.0, 49.5, 50.2, 50.8, 50.1, 51.0]}
r = {k: [math.log(v[i] / v[i - 1]) for i in range(1, 6)] for k, v in c.items()}
vol = {k: [None, None] + [st.stdev(x[i - 2 : i]) * math.sqrt(252) for i in range(2, 5)] for k, x in r.items()}
rows = []
for k, x in r.items():
    m = st.mean(x)
    s = st.stdev(x)
    ar, av = 252 * m, s * math.sqrt(252)
    w = pk = 1.0
    pkd = d[0]
    best = (0.0, d[0], d[0])
    for i, v in enumerate(x):
        w *= math.exp(v)
        if w > pk:
            pk, pkd = w, d[i + 1]
        if w / pk - 1 < best[0]:
            best = (w / pk - 1, pkd, d[i + 1])
    rows.append({"ticker": k, "ann_return": ar, "ann_vol": av, "sharpe": ar / av, "last_vol": vol[k][-1],
                 "max_drawdown": best[0], "peak_date": best[1], "trough_date": best[2]})
print(json.dumps(rows, indent=2))

D90Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/contracts/test_contracts.py (156 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/contracts/test_contracts.py
"""The frozen M1 contracts: each sample builds its type and holds its invariants; the fakes serve them."""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect
import math
import re
from pathlib import Path

import mdata
import pytest
import risk
from mdata import GapError, Prices, RawRow
from risk import Drawdown, Report, Returns, TickerSummary, VolSeries

ISO = re.compile(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}")
CSV = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "mdata.load_prices" / "prices.csv"


def as_returns(data: dict) -> Returns:
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_raw_row_sample(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    assert len(rows) == 8
    assert {r.ticker for r in rows} == {"AAA", "BBB"}
    assert all(ISO.fullmatch(r.date) for r in rows)
    assert [r.close for r in rows].count(None) == 1


def test_prices_sample(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    prices = Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["closes"].items()},
    )
    assert list(prices.dates) == sorted(prices.dates)
    assert list(prices.tickers) == sorted(prices.tickers)
    for ticker in prices.tickers:
        assert len(prices.closes[ticker]) == len(prices.dates)
        assert all(c > 0 for c in prices.closes[ticker])


def test_returns_sample_is_the_prices_log_returns(contract_sample):
    prices = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    returns = as_returns(contract_sample("risk.Returns"))
    assert returns.base_date == prices["dates"][0]
    assert list(returns.dates) == prices["dates"][1:]
    for ticker in returns.tickers:
        closes = prices["closes"][ticker]
        expected = [math.log(b / a) for a, b in zip(closes, closes[1:], strict=False)]
        assert returns.values[ticker] == pytest.approx(expected, abs=1e-15)


def test_vol_series_sample(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    vol = VolSeries(
        returns=as_returns(data["returns"]),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )
    assert vol.returns == as_returns(contract_sample("risk.Returns"))
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert vol.annualized is True
    for ticker in vol.returns.tickers:
        values = vol.values[ticker]
        assert len(values) == len(vol.returns.dates)
        assert values[: vol.window] == (None,) * vol.window
        assert all(v is not None and v >= 0 for v in values[vol.window :])


def test_ticker_summary_sample(contract_sample):
    summary = TickerSummary(**contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary"))
    assert summary.ticker == "AAA"
    assert summary.last_vol is not None
    assert summary.sharpe == pytest.approx(summary.ann_return / summary.ann_vol)


def test_drawdown_sample(contract_sample):
    drawdown = Drawdown(**contract_sample("risk.Drawdown"))
    assert drawdown.depth <= 0
    assert drawdown.peak_date < drawdown.trough_date


def test_report_sample(contract_sample):
    data = contract_sample("risk.Report")
    report = Report(path=data["path"], rows=tuple(data["rows"]))
    tickers = [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows]
    assert tickers == sorted(set(tickers))
    fields = set(TickerSummary.__dataclass_fields__) | {"max_drawdown", "peak_date", "trough_date"}
    assert all(set(row) == fields for row in report.rows)


def test_gap_error_is_a_value_error():
    assert issubclass(GapError, ValueError)


def test_load_prices_fake_serves_the_prices_sample(contract_fake, contract_sample):
    fake = contract_fake("mdata.load_prices")
    assert inspect.signature(fake.load_prices) == inspect.signature(mdata.load_prices)
    prices = fake.load_prices(CSV)
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    assert prices.dates == tuple(data["dates"])
    assert prices.tickers == tuple(data["tickers"])
    assert prices.closes == {t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["closes"].items()}
    assert fake.load_prices(CSV, ["BBB"]).tickers == ("BBB",)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="ZZZ"):
        fake.load_prices(CSV, ["ZZZ"])


def test_vol_series_fake_serves_the_vol_sample(contract_fake, contract_sample):
    fake = contract_fake("risk.vol_series")
    assert inspect.signature(fake.vol_series) == inspect.signature(risk.vol_series)
    vol = fake.vol_series(CSV, window=2)
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    assert vol.returns == as_returns(data["returns"])
    assert vol.annualized is True
    assert vol.values == {t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window"):
        fake.vol_series(CSV, window=1)


def test_the_csv_holds_the_prices_sample(contract_sample):
    lines = CSV.read_text().splitlines()
    assert lines[0] == "date,ticker,close"
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    closes: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    for line in lines[1:]:
        _, ticker, close = line.split(",")
        closes.setdefault(ticker, []).append(float(close))
    assert closes == data["closes"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("module", "names"),
    [
        (mdata, {"RawRow", "Prices", "GapError", "load_prices", "read_csv", "validate_rows", "align_calendar"}),
        (
            risk,
            {
                "Returns", "VolSeries", "TickerSummary", "Drawdown", "Report", "vol_series", "risk_report",
                "log_returns", "rolling_vol", "annualize", "summary_stats", "max_drawdown", "write_report",
            },
        ),
    ],
)
def test_exports_are_the_contract(module, names):
    assert set(module.__all__) == names

### mdata.RawRow: One CSV row
**Kind:** type · **Sample:** `tests/contracts/mdata.RawRow/sample.json` · **Cases:** eight rows of two tickers, one with a blank close
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RawRow: date: str; ticker: str; close: float | None`
- **Invariants:** `date` is ISO `yyyy-mm-dd`; `close` is None for a blank cell
- **Used by:** PL-01

<!-- docs/spec.md:11 -->
- **DATA-01** A `date,ticker,close` CSV is read row by row.
  - Given a CSV, when it is read, then each row comes back in file order, and a blank close is missing, not zero.

## Layout (`dt layout mdata`)
packages/mdata/src/mdata/
  __init__.py — mdata: daily closes from a CSV, validated and on one calendar.
  calendar.py — PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar.
  io.py — PL-01 step 1: read a ``date,ticker,close`` CSV row by row.
  pipeline.py — PL-01 entry: prices from a CSV.
  types.py — mdata's boundary types: one CSV row, the aligned prices, and the gap error.
  validate.py — PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, one per (date, ticker), sorted.

D105Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/io.py (31 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/io.py
"""PL-01 step 1: read a ``date,ticker,close`` CSV row by row."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import datetime as dt
from pathlib import Path

from mdata.types import RawRow

COLUMNS = ("date", "ticker", "close")


def read_csv(path: str | Path) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Every row in file order; a blank close is None. ValueError for a missing column or bad date."""
    with Path(path).open(newline="") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        missing = [c for c in COLUMNS if c not in (reader.fieldnames or ())]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing column(s) {', '.join(missing)}")
        return [_row(record, reader.line_num) for record in reader]


def _row(record: dict[str, str], line: int) -> RawRow:
    date = record["date"].strip()
    try:
        dt.date.fromisoformat(date)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"line {line}: malformed date {date!r}") from None
    close = record["close"].strip()
    return RawRow(date=date, ticker=record["ticker"].strip(), close=float(close) if close else None)

"""read_csv: every row in file order, a blank close is None, bad files are ValueErrors."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from mdata import RawRow, read_csv


def write(tmp_path, text: str):
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(text)
    return path


def test_reads_every_row_in_file_order(tmp_path):
    path = write(
        tmp_path,
        "date,ticker,close\n2024-03-04,BBB,49.5\n2024-03-01,AAA,100\n2024-03-04,BBB,0\n",
    )
    assert read_csv(path) == [
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "BBB", 49.5),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "AAA", 100.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "BBB", 0.0),
    ]


def test_blank_close_is_none_not_zero(tmp_path):
    path = write(tmp_path, "date,ticker,close\n2024-03-01,AAA,\n2024-03-01,BBB, \n")
    assert [row.close for row in read_csv(path)] == [None, None]


def test_column_order_does_not_matter(tmp_path):
    path = write(tmp_path, "ticker,close,date\nAAA,101.5,2024-03-05\n")
    assert read_csv(str(path)) == [RawRow("2024-03-05", "AAA", 101.5)]


def test_header_only_is_no_rows(tmp_path):
    assert read_csv(write(tmp_path, "date,ticker,close\n")) == []


def test_missing_column_is_a_value_error(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="close"):
        read_csv(write(tmp_path, "date,ticker\n2024-03-01,AAA\n"))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("date", ["2024/03/01", "2024-13-01", "yesterday"])
def test_malformed_date_is_a_value_error(tmp_path, date):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="malformed date"):
        read_csv(write(tmp_path, f"date,ticker,close\n{date},AAA,1\n"))

D115Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/validate.py (17 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/validate.py
"""PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, one per (date, ticker), sorted."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import RawRow


def validate_rows(rows: Sequence[RawRow], tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Positive closes only, the later duplicate wins, sorted by (date, ticker).

    KeyError for a requested ticker with no usable row.
    """
    wanted = set(tickers) if tickers is not None else None
    kept = [r for r in rows if r.close is not None and (wanted is None or r.ticker in wanted)]
    return sorted(kept, key=lambda r: (r.date, r.ticker))

"""validate_rows: rows with a close, sorted by (date, ticker), on the mdata.RawRow sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import RawRow, validate_rows


def sample_rows(contract_sample) -> list[RawRow]:
    return [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]


def test_drops_the_blank_close(contract_sample):
    rows = validate_rows(sample_rows(contract_sample))
    assert len(rows) == 7
    assert RawRow("2024-03-05", "BBB", None) not in rows
    assert all(r.close is not None for r in rows)


def test_sorted_by_date_then_ticker():
    rows = [
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "BBB", 2.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "BBB", 1.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "AAA", 3.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "AAA", 4.0),
    ]
    assert [(r.date, r.ticker) for r in validate_rows(rows)] == [
        ("2024-03-01", "AAA"),
        ("2024-03-01", "BBB"),
        ("2024-03-04", "AAA"),
        ("2024-03-04", "BBB"),
    ]


def test_requested_tickers_only(contract_sample):
    rows = validate_rows(sample_rows(contract_sample), ["AAA"])
    assert {r.ticker for r in rows} == {"AAA"}
    assert len(rows) == 4


def test_no_rows_is_no_rows():
    assert validate_rows([]) == []

D124Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py (26 lines)

"""PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import Prices, RawRow


def align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:
    """From the first date every ticker has a close; a gap takes the previous close.

    GapError naming the ticker when more than ``max_gap`` dates in a row are missing.
    """
    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        if row.close is not None:
            closes.setdefault(row.ticker, {})[row.date] = row.close
    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))
    shared = set.intersection(*(set(by_date) for by_date in closes.values())) if closes else set()
    dates = tuple(sorted(shared))
    return Prices(
        dates=dates,
        tickers=tickers,
        closes={t: tuple(closes[t][d] for d in dates) for t in tickers},
    )

"""align_calendar: one calendar of the dates every ticker has, on the mdata.RawRow sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import Prices, RawRow, align_calendar


def test_sample_aligns_on_the_dates_every_ticker_has(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert prices == Prices(
        dates=("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-06"),
        tickers=("AAA", "BBB"),
        closes={"AAA": (100.0, 102.0, 99.0), "BBB": (50.0, 49.5, 50.8)},
    )


def test_tickers_sorted_and_dates_ascending():
    rows = [
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "BBB", 2.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-04", "AAA", 3.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "BBB", 1.0),
        RawRow("2024-03-01", "AAA", 4.0),
    ]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert prices.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    assert prices.dates == ("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04")
    assert prices.closes == {"AAA": (4.0, 3.0), "BBB": (1.0, 2.0)}


def test_one_ticker_keeps_all_its_dates():
    rows = [RawRow("2024-03-01", "AAA", 1.0), RawRow("2024-03-04", "AAA", 2.0)]
    assert align_calendar(rows).dates == ("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04")


def test_no_rows_is_empty_prices():
    assert align_calendar([]) == Prices(dates=(), tickers=(), closes={})

D132Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/returns.py (23 lines)

"""PL-02 step 2: daily returns from closes."""

from __future__ import annotations

import itertools

from mdata.types import Prices
from risk.types import Returns


def log_returns(prices: Prices) -> Returns:
    """r_t = ln(close_t / close_t-1), dated t. ValueError with fewer than two dates."""
    if len(prices.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"returns need at least two dates, got {len(prices.dates)}")
    return Returns(
        base_date=prices.dates[0],
        dates=prices.dates[1:],
        tickers=prices.tickers,
        values={
            t: tuple(b / a - 1 for a, b in itertools.pairwise(prices.closes[t]))
            for t in prices.tickers
        },
    )

"""log_returns: one return per date after the first, per ticker, on the mdata.Prices sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import Prices
from risk import log_returns


def sample_prices(contract_sample) -> Prices:
    data = contract_sample("mdata.Prices")
    return Prices(
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["closes"].items()},
    )


def test_dated_after_the_base_date(contract_sample):
    returns = log_returns(sample_prices(contract_sample))
    assert returns.base_date == "2024-03-01"
    assert returns.dates == ("2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-06", "2024-03-07", "2024-03-08")
    assert returns.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    assert all(len(returns.values[t]) == 5 for t in returns.tickers)


def test_flat_closes_return_zero():
    prices = Prices(dates=("d1", "d2", "d3"), tickers=("A",), closes={"A": (5.0, 5.0, 5.0)})
    assert log_returns(prices).values == {"A": (0.0, 0.0)}


def test_a_rise_is_positive_and_a_fall_negative():
    prices = Prices(dates=("d1", "d2", "d3"), tickers=("A",), closes={"A": (100.0, 110.0, 99.0)})
    up, down = log_returns(prices).values["A"]
    assert up > 0
    assert down < 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("dates", [(), ("2024-03-01",)])
def test_fewer_than_two_dates_is_a_value_error(dates):
    prices = Prices(dates=dates, tickers=("A",), closes={"A": tuple(1.0 for _ in dates)})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="two dates"):
        log_returns(prices)

"""PL-02 step 3: rolling volatility from earlier returns."""

from __future__ import annotations

import statistics

from risk.types import Returns, VolSeries


def rolling_vol(returns: Returns, window: int) -> VolSeries:
    """Sample stdev of the ``window`` returns before each date. ValueError if ``window`` < 2."""
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=window,
        annualized=False,
        values={t: _expanding(returns.values[t], window) for t in returns.tickers},
    )


def _expanding(values: tuple[float, ...], window: int) -> tuple[float | None, ...]:
    return tuple(
        statistics.stdev(values[:i]) if i >= window else None for i in range(len(values))
    )

"""rolling_vol: a daily volatility per return date from earlier returns, on the risk.Returns sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, rolling_vol


def sample_returns(contract_sample) -> Returns:
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_aligned_to_the_returns_and_none_until_the_window(contract_sample):
    returns = sample_returns(contract_sample)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, 2)
    assert vol.returns == returns
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert vol.annualized is False
    for ticker in returns.tickers:
        values = vol.values[ticker]
        assert len(values) == len(returns.dates)
        assert values[:2] == (None, None)
        assert all(v is not None and v > 0 for v in values[2:])


def test_uses_only_earlier_returns():
    # The return on each date is huge; it must not move that date's volatility.
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.03, 9.0)})
    assert rolling_vol(returns, 2).values["A"] == (None, None, pytest.approx(0.02 ** 0.5 / 10))


def test_constant_returns_have_zero_volatility():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3", "d4"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01,) * 4})
    assert rolling_vol(returns, 3).values["A"] == (None, None, None, 0.0)

"""PL-02 step 4: annualize a daily volatility."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

from risk.types import VolSeries


def annualize(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> VolSeries:
    """Each value times sqrt(periods_per_year). ValueError if ``vol`` is already annualized."""
    return dataclasses.replace(vol, annualized=True)

"""annualize: a VolSeries marked annualized, aligned as before, on the risk.VolSeries sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, annualize


def daily_sample(contract_sample) -> VolSeries:
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    r = data["returns"]
    return VolSeries(
        returns=Returns(
            base_date=r["base_date"],
            dates=tuple(r["dates"]),
            tickers=tuple(r["tickers"]),
            values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in r["values"].items()},
        ),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=False,
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_marks_the_series_annualized(contract_sample):
    vol = daily_sample(contract_sample)
    out = annualize(vol)
    assert out.annualized is True
    assert out.returns == vol.returns
    assert out.window == vol.window


def test_keeps_alignment_and_gaps(contract_sample):
    vol = daily_sample(contract_sample)
    out = annualize(vol)
    for ticker in vol.returns.tickers:
        assert len(out.values[ticker]) == len(vol.values[ticker])
        assert out.values[ticker][:2] == (None, None)
        assert all(v is not None and v >= 0 for v in out.values[ticker][2:])

"""PL-03 step 2: each ticker's whole-period summary."""

from __future__ import annotations

import statistics

from risk.types import TickerSummary, VolSeries


def summary_stats(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> tuple[TickerSummary, ...]:
    """Annualized return, volatility, Sharpe and the last rolling vol per ticker.

    ValueError with fewer than two returns.
    """
    if len(vol.returns.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"a summary needs at least two returns, got {len(vol.returns.dates)}")
    return tuple(_summary(vol, ticker) for ticker in vol.returns.tickers)


def _summary(vol: VolSeries, ticker: str) -> TickerSummary:
    values = vol.returns.values[ticker]
    mean = statistics.mean(values)
    stdev = statistics.stdev(values)
    return TickerSummary(
        ticker=ticker,
        ann_return=mean,
        ann_vol=stdev,
        sharpe=mean / stdev if stdev else 0.0,
        last_vol=vol.values[ticker][-1],
    )

D160Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/unit/risk/test_summary.py (54 lines)

"""summary_stats: one TickerSummary per ticker, on the risk.VolSeries sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, summary_stats


def sample_vol(contract_sample) -> VolSeries:
    data = contract_sample("risk.VolSeries")
    r = data["returns"]
    return VolSeries(
        returns=Returns(
            base_date=r["base_date"],
            dates=tuple(r["dates"]),
            tickers=tuple(r["tickers"]),
            values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in r["values"].items()},
        ),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_one_summary_per_ticker_in_order(contract_sample):
    vol = sample_vol(contract_sample)
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    assert [s.ticker for s in summary] == ["AAA", "BBB"]
    for s in summary:
        assert s.last_vol == vol.values[s.ticker][-1]
        assert s.ann_vol > 0
        assert s.sharpe == pytest.approx(s.ann_return / s.ann_vol)


def test_zero_volatility_has_zero_sharpe():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.01, 0.01)})
    vol = VolSeries(returns, 2, True, {"A": (None, None, 0.0)})
    (s,) = summary_stats(vol)
    assert s.ann_vol == 0.0
    assert s.sharpe == 0.0
    assert s.last_vol == 0.0


def test_last_vol_may_be_none():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.02)})
    vol = VolSeries(returns, 2, True, {"A": (None, None)})
    assert summary_stats(vol)[0].last_vol is None


def test_fewer_than_two_returns_is_a_value_error():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1",), ("A",), {"A": (0.01,)})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="two returns"):
        summary_stats(VolSeries(returns, 2, True, {"A": (None,)}))
full input and output
input

"""PL-03 step 3: each ticker's maximum drawdown."""

from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
import math

from risk.types import Drawdown, Returns


def max_drawdown(returns: Returns) -> tuple[Drawdown, ...]:
    """The deepest fall below the running peak of wealth, which starts at 1 on ``base_date``."""
    return tuple(_drawdown(returns, ticker) for ticker in returns.tickers)


def _drawdown(returns: Returns, ticker: str) -> Drawdown:
    wealth = [math.exp(s) for s in itertools.accumulate(returns.values[ticker], initial=0.0)]
    peaks = itertools.accumulate(wealth, max)
    depth = min(w / p - 1 for w, p in zip(wealth, peaks, strict=True))
    return Drawdown(
        ticker=ticker, depth=depth, peak_date=returns.base_date, trough_date=returns.base_date
    )

"""max_drawdown: one Drawdown per ticker, depth at most 0, on the risk.Returns sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pytest

from risk import Returns, max_drawdown


def sample_returns(contract_sample) -> Returns:
    data = contract_sample("risk.Returns")
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_one_drawdown_per_ticker_at_most_zero(contract_sample):
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(sample_returns(contract_sample))
    assert [d.ticker for d in drawdowns] == ["AAA", "BBB"]
    assert all(-1 < d.depth < 0 for d in drawdowns)


def test_depth_is_the_fall_from_the_running_peak():
    # wealth 1 -> 1.2 -> 0.9 -> 1.5: the fall is 0.9 / 1.2 - 1 = -0.25
    values = (math.log(1.2), math.log(0.9 / 1.2), math.log(1.5 / 0.9))
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": values})
    assert max_drawdown(returns)[0].depth == pytest.approx(-0.25, abs=1e-12)


def test_a_rising_series_has_no_drawdown():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.02)})
    assert max_drawdown(returns)[0].depth == 0.0


def test_a_first_day_fall_counts_from_the_base():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (math.log(0.9), math.log(1.05))})
    assert max_drawdown(returns)[0].depth == pytest.approx(-0.1, abs=1e-12)

D176Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report.py (36 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report.py
"""PL-03 step 4: write the JSON risk report."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk.types import Drawdown, Report, TickerSummary


def write_report(
    summary: Sequence[TickerSummary], drawdowns: Sequence[Drawdown], out_path: str | Path
) -> Report:
    """Write ``{"tickers": [...]}``, one row per ticker, sorted.

    ValueError if summary and drawdowns cover different tickers.
    """
    by_ticker = {d.ticker: d for d in drawdowns}
    if sorted(by_ticker) != sorted(s.ticker for s in summary) or len(by_ticker) != len(drawdowns):
        raise ValueError("summary and drawdowns cover different tickers")
    rows = tuple(
        _row(s, by_ticker[s.ticker]) for s in sorted(summary, key=lambda s: s.ticker)
    )
    Path(out_path).write_text(json.dumps({"tickers": rows}, indent=2) + "\n")
    return Report(path=str(out_path), rows=rows)


def _row(summary: TickerSummary, drawdown: Drawdown) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        **dataclasses.asdict(summary),
        "max_drawdown": drawdown.depth,
        "peak_date": drawdown.peak_date,
        "trough_date": drawdown.trough_date,
    }

D177Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/unit/risk/test_report.py (55 lines)

"""write_report: the JSON rows, one per ticker, sorted, the same in the file and the Report."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from risk import Drawdown, TickerSummary, write_report


def summaries(contract_sample) -> list[TickerSummary]:
    aaa = TickerSummary(**contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary"))
    zzz = TickerSummary("ZZZ", 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, None)
    return [zzz, aaa]


def drawdowns() -> list[Drawdown]:
    return [
        Drawdown("AAA", -0.03, "2024-03-04", "2024-03-06"),
        Drawdown("ZZZ", 0.0, "2024-03-01", "2024-03-01"),
    ]


def test_rows_sorted_with_every_field(contract_sample, tmp_path):
    report = write_report(summaries(contract_sample), drawdowns(), tmp_path / "out.json")
    sample = contract_sample("risk.TickerSummary")
    assert report.path == str(tmp_path / "out.json")
    assert [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows] == ["AAA", "ZZZ"]
    assert report.rows[0] == {
        **sample,
        "max_drawdown": -0.03,
        "peak_date": "2024-03-04",
        "trough_date": "2024-03-06",
    }
    assert report.rows[1]["last_vol"] is None


def test_file_holds_the_same_rows(contract_sample, tmp_path):
    out = tmp_path / "out.json"
    report = write_report(summaries(contract_sample), drawdowns(), str(out))
    assert json.loads(out.read_text()) == {"tickers": [dict(row) for row in report.rows]}


def test_same_input_same_bytes(contract_sample, tmp_path):
    a, b = tmp_path / "a.json", tmp_path / "b.json"
    write_report(summaries(contract_sample), drawdowns(), a)
    write_report(list(reversed(summaries(contract_sample))), list(reversed(drawdowns())), b)
    assert a.read_bytes() == b.read_bytes()


def test_different_tickers_is_a_value_error(contract_sample, tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="different tickers"):
        write_report(summaries(contract_sample), drawdowns()[:1], tmp_path / "out.json")
    assert not (tmp_path / "out.json").exists()

"""PL-01 entry: prices from a CSV."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from mdata.calendar import align_calendar
from mdata.io import read_csv
from mdata.types import Prices
from mdata.validate import validate_rows


def load_prices(path: str | Path, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Prices:
    """Read, validate and align the CSV at ``path`` (DATA-01-DATA-04).

    KeyError for a requested ticker with no usable row; GapError for a gap over 5 dates;
    ValueError for a malformed file.
    """
    rows = read_csv(path)
    rows = validate_rows(rows, tickers)
    return align_calendar(rows)

"""PL-01 smoke: load_prices runs end to end on a CSV; shape only, the golden cases check values."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from mdata import Prices, load_prices

CSV = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "contracts" / "mdata.load_prices" / "prices.csv"


def test_pl01_runs_end_to_end():
    prices = load_prices(CSV)
    assert isinstance(prices, Prices)
    assert prices.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    assert len(prices.dates) > 0
    assert all(len(prices.closes[t]) == len(prices.dates) for t in prices.tickers)


def test_pl01_picks_the_requested_tickers():
    prices = load_prices(str(CSV), ["BBB"])
    assert prices.tickers == ("BBB",)
    assert set(prices.closes) == {"BBB"}

D199Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol_pipeline.py (25 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol_pipeline.py
"""PL-02 entry: annualized rolling volatility of a CSV."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import mdata
from risk.annualize import annualize
from risk.returns import log_returns
from risk.types import VolSeries
from risk.vol import rolling_vol


def vol_series(
    path: str | Path, *, window: int = 20, tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None
) -> VolSeries:
    """Load the prices, take log returns, roll and annualize (RISK-01-RISK-03).

    Errors as ``mdata.load_prices``; ValueError if ``window`` < 2.
    """
    prices = mdata.load_prices(path, tickers)  # late-bound: a fake can stand in
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, window)
    return annualize(vol)

"""PL-02 smoke: vol_series runs end to end on the mdata.load_prices fake; shape only."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

import mdata
from risk import VolSeries, vol_series


@pytest.fixture
def fake_prices(monkeypatch, contract_fake):
    fake = contract_fake("mdata.load_prices")
    monkeypatch.setattr(mdata, "load_prices", fake.load_prices)
    return fake


def test_pl02_runs_end_to_end(fake_prices):
    vol = vol_series(fake_prices.CSV, window=2)
    assert isinstance(vol, VolSeries)
    assert vol.annualized is True
    assert vol.window == 2
    assert vol.returns.tickers == ("AAA", "BBB")
    n = len(vol.returns.dates)
    assert n > 0
    assert all(len(vol.values[t]) == n for t in vol.returns.tickers)


def test_pl02_passes_the_tickers_through(fake_prices):
    vol = vol_series(fake_prices.CSV, window=2, tickers=["AAA"])
    assert vol.returns.tickers == ("AAA",)
    assert set(vol.values) == {"AAA"}

D210Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/report_pipeline.py (26 lines)

"""PL-03 entry: the JSON risk report of a CSV."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from risk import vol_pipeline
from risk.drawdown import max_drawdown
from risk.report import write_report
from risk.summary import summary_stats
from risk.types import Report


def risk_report(
    path: str | Path,
    out_path: str | Path,
    *,
    window: int = 20,
    tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None,
) -> Report:
    """Volatility, summary and drawdowns of the CSV at ``path``, written to ``out_path`` (REP-01)."""
    vol = vol_pipeline.vol_series(path, window=window, tickers=tickers)  # late-bound for a fake
    summary = summary_stats(vol)
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(vol.returns)
    return write_report(summary, drawdowns, out_path)

"""PL-03 smoke: risk_report runs end to end on the risk.vol_series fake; shape only."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from risk import Report, risk_report, vol_pipeline


@pytest.fixture
def fake_vol(monkeypatch, contract_fake):
    fake = contract_fake("risk.vol_series")
    monkeypatch.setattr(vol_pipeline, "vol_series", fake.vol_series)
    return fake


def test_pl03_runs_end_to_end(fake_vol, tmp_path):
    out = tmp_path / "report.json"
    report = risk_report(fake_vol.CSV, out, window=2)
    assert isinstance(report, Report)
    assert report.path == str(out)
    assert [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows] == ["AAA", "BBB"]
    assert len(json.loads(out.read_text())["tickers"]) == 2


def test_pl03_passes_the_tickers_through(fake_vol, tmp_path):
    report = risk_report(fake_vol.CSV, tmp_path / "report.json", window=2, tickers=["BBB"])
    assert [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows] == ["BBB"]

full input and output
input
{
  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/scripts/red_check.py"
}
output
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""RED for the right reason: the tester's new intent tests fail on the behaviour, not the wiring.
3	
4	Called by `scripts/check.sh collect` with the intent test files changed since the task's base.
5	Runs them once and sorts every test by why it ended:
6	
7	    right  AssertionError, NotImplementedError (an interface stub, `raise
8	           NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`) or pytest.fail / pytest.raises' "DID NOT RAISE"
9	    wrong  anything else: ImportError, AttributeError, NameError, TypeError, a fixture or
10	           collection error. The test can't reach the behaviour it claims to check.
11	    passed the code already does this; listed, not a failure
12	
13	Exits 1 when any test is wrong, or when none is red: a test that can't fail proves nothing.
14	With no changed test file there is nothing to judge, and it exits 0.
15	Prints one line per test that is not right, as `nodeid: reason`.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import importlib
21	import sys
22	from typing import Any
23	
24	RIGHT = {"AssertionError", "NotImplementedError", "Failed"}  # Failed: pytest.fail, DID NOT RAISE
25	
26	
27	def _right(exc: BaseException | None) -> bool:
28	    return exc is not None and bool({c.__name__ for c in type(exc).__mro__} & RIGHT)
29	
30	
31	class Recorder:
32	    """A pytest plugin that keeps how each test ended: right, wrong (with why) or passed."""
33	
34	    def __init__(self) -> None:
35	        self.results: dict[str, str] = {}
36	
37	    def pytest_collectreport(self, report: Any) -> None:
38	        if report.failed:
39	            self.results[report.nodeid or "collection"] = "wrong: collection error"
40	
41	    def pytest_exception_interact(self, node: Any, call: Any, report: Any) -> None:
42	        if not hasattr(node, "function"):
43	            return  # a collector: pytest_collectreport has it
44	        exc = call.excinfo.value if call.excinfo else None
45	        if call.when == "call" and _right(exc):
46	            self.results[node.nodeid] = "right"
47	        else:
48	            self.results[node.nodeid] = f"wrong: {call.when}: {type(exc).__name__}"
49	
50	    def pytest_runtest_logreport(self, report: Any) -> None:
51	        if report.when == "call" and report.passed:
52	            self.results.setdefault(report.nodeid, "passed")
53	
54	
55	def main(paths: list[str]) -> int:
56	    if not paths:
57	        print("red_check: no intent test changed since the base; nothing to check", file=sys.stderr)
58	        return 0
59	    # pytest is a dev dependency, present wherever check.sh runs. Imported by name: deptry audits
60	    # the product's imports, and this script is the bar's.
61	    pytest = importlib.import_module("pytest")
62	    recorder = Recorder()
63	    # -m "": every marker, so a slow or perf intent test the default addopts deselect is judged too.
64	    pytest.main(
65	        [*paths, "-q", "--no-header", "--no-cov", "-m", "", "-p", "no:cacheprovider"], [recorder]
66	    )
67	    results = recorder.results
68	    wrong = {n: r for n, r in results.items() if r.startswith("wrong")}
69	    for nodeid, why in sorted(wrong.items()):
70	        print(f"{nodeid}: fails for the wrong reason ({why.removeprefix('wrong: ')})")
71	    for nodeid in sorted(n for n, r in results.items() if r == "passed"):
72	        print(f"{nodeid}: passes already (not red)")
73	    if wrong:
74	        print("red_check: fix the tests above: each must reach the code and fail on its behaviour")
75	        return 1
76	    red = sum(r == "right" for r in results.values())
77	    if not red:
78	        print("red_check: no test is red; a test that can't fail proves nothing")
79	        return 1
80	    print(f"red_check: {red} red for the right reason", file=sys.stderr)
81	    return 0
82	
83	
84	if __name__ == "__main__":
85	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
86

D222Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/intent/mdata/test_validate.py (32 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/intent/mdata/test_validate.py
"""DATA-02: unusable rows dropped, duplicates resolved, rows sorted (TC-01); a missing ticker is a KeyError."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import RawRow, validate_rows

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def test_tc01_unusable_rows_and_duplicates(golden):
    given, expected = golden("mdata", "TC-01")
    rows = [RawRow(date, ticker, close) for date, ticker, close in given["rows"]]
    result = validate_rows(rows, given["tickers"])
    assert result == [RawRow(date, ticker, close) for date, ticker, close in expected["rows"]]


def test_requested_ticker_with_no_usable_row_is_a_key_error(golden):
    given, _ = golden("mdata", "TC-01")
    rows = [RawRow(date, ticker, close) for date, ticker, close in given["rows"]]
    unusable = [r for r in rows if r.ticker == "B" and (r.close is None or r.close <= 0)]
    usable_a = [r for r in rows if r.ticker == "A"]
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="B"):
        validate_rows(usable_a + unusable, ["A", "B"])


def test_requested_ticker_absent_from_the_file_is_a_key_error(golden):
    given, _ = golden("mdata", "TC-01")
    rows = [RawRow(date, ticker, close) for date, ticker, close in given["rows"]]
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="ZZZ"):
        validate_rows(rows, ["A", "ZZZ"])

"""PL-01 step 2: keep the usable rows, one per (date, ticker), sorted."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import RawRow


def validate_rows(rows: Sequence[RawRow], tickers: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[RawRow]:
    """Positive closes only, the later duplicate wins, sorted by (date, ticker).

    KeyError for a requested ticker with no usable row.
    """
    wanted = set(tickers) if tickers is not None else None
    latest: dict[tuple[str, str], RawRow] = {}
    for row in rows:
        if row.close is not None and row.close > 0 and (wanted is None or row.ticker in wanted):
            latest[row.date, row.ticker] = row
    missing = sorted((wanted or set()) - {ticker for _, ticker in latest})
    if missing:
        raise KeyError(f"no usable row for ticker(s) {', '.join(missing)}")
    return [latest[key] for key in sorted(latest)]

"""DATA-03: one calendar, gaps take the previous close (TC-02); DATA-04: a long gap is a GapError (TC-03)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

import mdata
from mdata import Prices, RawRow, align_calendar

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def rows_of(given: dict) -> list[RawRow]:
    return [RawRow(date, ticker, close) for date, ticker, close in given["rows"]]


def test_tc02_one_calendar_with_filled_gaps(golden):
    given, expected = golden("mdata", "TC-02")
    prices = align_calendar(rows_of(given), max_gap=given["max_gap"])
    assert prices == Prices(
        dates=tuple(expected["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(expected["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(v) for t, v in expected["closes"].items()},
    )


def test_tc03_a_gap_over_the_limit_is_a_gap_error(golden):
    given, expected = golden("mdata", "TC-03")
    error = getattr(mdata, expected["raises"])
    with pytest.raises(error, match=expected["match"]):
        align_calendar(rows_of(given), max_gap=given["max_gap"])

D245Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py (45 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py
"""PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

from mdata.types import GapError, Prices, RawRow


def align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:
    """From the first date every ticker has a close; a gap takes the previous close.

    GapError naming the ticker when more than ``max_gap`` dates in a row are missing.
    """
    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        if row.close is not None:
            closes.setdefault(row.ticker, {})[row.date] = row.close
    shared = set.intersection(*(set(by_date) for by_date in closes.values())) if closes else set()
    if not shared:
        return Prices(dates=(), tickers=tuple(sorted(closes)), closes={t: () for t in closes})
    start = min(shared)
    dates = tuple(sorted({d for by_date in closes.values() for d in by_date if d >= start}))
    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))
    return Prices(
        dates=dates,
        tickers=tickers,
        closes={t: _filled(t, closes[t], dates, max_gap) for t in tickers},
    )


def _filled(ticker: str, by_date: dict[str, float], dates: tuple[str, ...], max_gap: int) -> tuple[float, ...]:
    """The ticker's closes on ``dates``, each gap taking the previous close; dates[0] has a close."""
    filled: list[float] = []
    missing = 0
    for date in dates:
        if date in by_date:
            filled.append(by_date[date])
            missing = 0
            continue
        missing += 1
        if missing > max_gap:
            raise GapError(f"{ticker}: more than {max_gap} dates in a row missing, up to {date}")
        filled.append(filled[-1])
    return tuple(filled)
full input and output
input

  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/mdata/src/mdata/calendar.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"PL-01 step 3: put every ticker on one calendar.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom collections.abc import Sequence\n\nfrom mdata.types import GapError, Prices, RawRow\n\n\ndef align_calendar(rows: Sequence[RawRow], *, max_gap: int = 5) -> Prices:\n    \"\"\"From the first date every ticker has a close; a gap takes the previous close.\n\n    GapError naming the ticker when more than ``max_gap`` dates in a row are missing.\n    \"\"\"\n    closes: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    for row in rows:\n        if row.close is not None:\n            closes.setdefault(row.ticker, {})[row.date] = row.close\n    shared = set.intersection(*(set(by_date) for by_date in closes.values())) if closes else set()\n    if not shared:\n        return Prices(dates=(), tickers=tuple(sorted(closes)), closes={t: () for t in closes})\n    start = min(shared)\n    dates = tuple(sorted({d for by_date in closes.values() for d in by_date if d >= start}))\n    tickers = tuple(sorted(closes))\n    return Prices(\n        dates=dates,\n        tickers=tickers,\n        closes={t: _filled(t, closes[t], dates, max_gap) for t in tickers},\n    )\n\n\ndef _filled(ticker: str, by_date: dict[str, float], dates: tuple[str, ...], max_gap: int) -> tuple[float, ...]:\n    \"\"\"The ticker's closes on ``dates``, each gap taking the previous close; dates[0] has a close.\"\"\"\n    filled: list[float] = []\n    missing = 0\n    for date in dates:\n        if date in by_date:\n            filled.append(by_date[date])\n            missing = 0\n            continue\n        missing += 1\n        if missing > max_gap:\n            raise GapError(f\"{ticker}: more than {max_gap} dates in a row missing, up to {date}\")\n        filled.append(filled[-1])\n    return tuple(filled)\n"
}

"""align_calendar: one calendar of the dates every ticker has, on the mdata.RawRow sample."""

from __future__ import annotations

from mdata import Prices, RawRow, align_calendar


def test_sample_aligns_on_the_dates_every_ticker_has(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]

new
"""align_calendar: one calendar, gaps take the previous close, a long gap is a GapError."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import GapError, Prices, RawRow, align_calendar


def test_sample_fills_the_blank_close_with_the_previous_one(contract_sample):
    rows = [RawRow(**r) for r in contract_sample("mdata.RawRow")["rows"]]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert prices == Prices(
        dates=("2024-03-01", "2024-03-04", "2024-03-05", "2024-03-06"),
        tickers=("AAA", "BBB"),
        closes={"AAA": (100.0, 102.0, 101.0, 99.0), "BBB": (50.0, 49.5, 49.5, 50.8)},
    )


def test_starts_on_the_first_date_every_ticker_has():
    rows = [
        RawRow("d1", "A", 1.0),
        RawRow("d2", "A", 2.0),
        RawRow("d2", "B", 5.0),
        RawRow("d3", "A", 3.0),
    ]
    prices = align_calendar(rows)
    assert prices.dates == ("d2", "d3")
    assert prices.closes == {"A": (2.0, 3.0), "B": (5.0, 5.0)}


def test_a_gap_of_exactly_max_gap_is_filled():
    rows = [RawRow(f"d{i}", "A", float(i)) for i in range(5)]
    rows += [RawRow("d0", "B", 9.0), RawRow("d4", "B", 8.0)]
    assert align_calendar(rows, max_gap=3).closes["B"] == (9.0, 9.0, 9.0, 9.0, 8.0)


def test_a_gap_one_over_max_gap_names_the_ticker():
    rows = [RawRow(f"d{i}", "A", float(i)) for i in range(5)]
    rows += [RawRow("d0", "B", 9.0), RawRow("d4", "B", 8.0)]
    with pytest.raises(GapError, match="B"):
        align_calendar(rows, max_gap=2)


def test_a_trailing_gap_counts_too():
    rows = [RawRow(f"d{i}", "A", float(i)) for i in range(4)] + [RawRow("d0", "B", 9.0)]
    with pytest.raises(GapError, match="B"):
        align_calendar(rows, max_gap=2)


def test_no_shared_date_is_no_dates():
    prices = align_calendar([RawRow("d1", "A", 1.0), RawRow("d2", "B", 2.0)])
    assert prices.dates == ()
    assert prices.closes == {"A": (), "B": ()}

"""RISK-01: daily returns are log returns, dated t (TC-04)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from mdata import Prices
from risk import log_returns

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def test_tc04_geometric_closes(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-04")
    p = given["prices"]
    prices = Prices(
        dates=tuple(p["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(p["tickers"]),
        closes={t: tuple(v) for t, v in p["closes"].items()},
    )
    returns = log_returns(prices)
    tol = expected["tol"]
    assert returns.base_date == expected["base_date"]
    assert returns.dates == tuple(expected["dates"])
    assert returns.tickers == tuple(expected["tickers"])
    for ticker, values in expected["values"].items():
        assert returns.values[ticker] == pytest.approx(values, abs=tol, rel=0)

<!-- docs/packages/risk/tests.md:16 -->
### TC-05: Rolling volatility from earlier returns
**Pipeline:** PL-02 · **Covers:** RISK-02 · **Kind:** synthetic known-answer · **Fixture:** tests/fixtures/risk/TC-05/
**Source:** eight hand-made returns, window 4; sample standard deviation of the four returns before each date
**Expected:** no value for the first four dates, then the listed values · **Tolerance:** 1e-12 absolute

<!-- docs/spec.md:27 -->
- **RISK-02** Rolling volatility uses only earlier returns.
  - Given a window of w, when the volatility for date t is computed, then it is the sample standard deviation (n − 1) of the w returns before t, never t's own return, and there is no value until w earlier returns exist.

### Reference RF-01: references/vol_notebook.py
> Reference, not part of the project. Reuse what fits the contract and step table as is, adapt it, or ignore it: your call.
> Never import from references/. Anything you bring over meets the same bar as the rest of the code.
> Caveats: pandas and numpy, which the project doesn't use (AD-01): reimplement, don't copy
```python
"""My old notebook code for volatility and drawdown (pandas). Kept for the formulas, not to import."""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


def daily_returns(prices: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Log returns of a dates x tickers close panel."""
    return np.log(prices / prices.shift(1)).dropna(how="all")


def rolling_vol(returns: pd.DataFrame, window: int = 20) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Rolling volatility over the last `window` days, today included."""
    return returns.rolling(window).apply(lambda x: np.std(x), raw=True)


def annualize(vol: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Annualize to a calendar year, like my brokerage statements: 365 days."""
    return vol * np.sqrt(365)


def summary(returns: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Yearly return and volatility per ticker."""
    return pd.DataFrame({"ann_return": returns.mean() * 365, "ann_vol": returns.std(ddof=0) * np.sqrt(365)})


def max_drawdown(prices: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:
    """Deepest fall below the running high."""
    return (prices / prices.cummax() - 1).min()
```

"""RISK-02: rolling volatility is the sample stdev of the w returns before t (TC-05); w < 2 is an error."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, rolling_vol

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def returns_of(data: dict) -> Returns:
    return Returns(
        base_date=data["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(data["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(data["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_tc05_rolling_volatility_from_earlier_returns(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-05")
    returns = returns_of(given["returns"])
    vol = rolling_vol(returns, given["window"])
    tol = expected["tol"]
    assert vol.window == expected["window"]
    assert vol.annualized is expected["annualized"]
    assert vol.returns == returns
    for ticker, values in expected["values"].items():
        got = vol.values[ticker]
        assert len(got) == len(values)
        for g, e in zip(got, values, strict=True):
            if e is None:
                assert g is None
            else:
                assert g == pytest.approx(e, abs=tol, rel=0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("window", [1, 0, -3])
def test_window_below_two_is_a_value_error(golden, window):
    given, _ = golden("risk", "TC-05")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window"):
        rolling_vol(returns_of(given["returns"]), window)

D281Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol.py (27 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/vol.py
"""PL-02 step 3: rolling volatility from earlier returns."""

from __future__ import annotations

import statistics

from risk.types import Returns, VolSeries


def rolling_vol(returns: Returns, window: int) -> VolSeries:
    """Sample stdev of the ``window`` returns before each date. ValueError if ``window`` < 2."""
    if window < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"window must be at least 2, got {window}")
    return VolSeries(
        returns=returns,
        window=window,
        annualized=False,
        values={t: _rolling(returns.values[t], window) for t in returns.tickers},
    )


def _rolling(values: tuple[float, ...], window: int) -> tuple[float | None, ...]:
    """Date i's value is the stdev of returns i-window .. i-1, never return i itself."""
    return tuple(
        statistics.stdev(values[i - window : i]) if i >= window else None
        for i in range(len(values))
    )

ValueError if ``window`` < 2.\"\"\"\n    if window < 2:\n        raise ValueError(f\"window must be at least 2, got {window}\")\n    return VolSeries(\n        returns=returns,\n        window=window,\n        annualized=False,\n        values={t: _rolling(returns.values[t], window) for t in returns.tickers},\n    )\n\n\ndef _rolling(values: tuple[float, ...], window: int) -> tuple[float | None, ...]:\n    \"\"\"Date i's value is the stdev of returns i-window .. i-1, never return i itself.\"\"\"\n    return tuple(\n        statistics.stdev(values[i - window : i]) if i >= window else None\n        for i in range(len(values))\n    )\n"

def test_the_window_rolls_and_forgets_older_returns():
    # d4 sees only (0.01, 0.01): an expanding window would still include the 5.0
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3", "d4"), ("A",), {"A": (5.0, 0.01, 0.01, 7.0)})
    assert rolling_vol(returns, 2).values["A"][3] == 0.0


def test_window_one_is_a_value_error():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.02)})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 2, got 1"):
        rolling_vol(returns, 1)

"""RISK-03: volatility is annualized over 252 trading days (TC-06); annualizing twice is an error."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, annualize

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def vol_of(data: dict) -> VolSeries:
    r = data["returns"]
    return VolSeries(
        returns=Returns(
            base_date=r["base_date"],
            dates=tuple(r["dates"]),
            tickers=tuple(r["tickers"]),
            values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in r["values"].items()},
        ),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_tc06_annualizing(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-06")
    vol = vol_of(given["vol"])
    out = annualize(vol)
    tol = expected["tol"]
    assert out.annualized is expected["annualized"]
    assert out.returns == vol.returns
    assert out.window == vol.window
    for ticker, values in expected["values"].items():
        got = out.values[ticker]
        assert len(got) == len(values)
        for g, e in zip(got, values, strict=True):
            if e is None:
                assert g is None
            else:
                assert g == pytest.approx(e, abs=tol, rel=0)


def test_annualizing_an_annualized_series_is_a_value_error(golden):
    given, _ = golden("risk", "TC-06")
    already = dataclasses.replace(vol_of(given["vol"]), annualized=True)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="annualized"):
        annualize(already)

"""PL-02 step 4: annualize a daily volatility."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import math

from risk.types import VolSeries


def annualize(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> VolSeries:
    """Each value times sqrt(periods_per_year). ValueError if ``vol`` is already annualized."""
    if vol.annualized:
        raise ValueError("the series is already annualized")
    scale = math.sqrt(periods_per_year)
    values = {t: tuple(None if v is None else v * scale for v in vs) for t, vs in vol.values.items()}
    return dataclasses.replace(vol, annualized=True, values=values)

def test_scales_by_the_root_of_the_periods():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.0, 0.0)})
    vol = VolSeries(returns, 2, False, {"A": (None, 0.5)})
    assert annualize(vol).values["A"] == (None, pytest.approx(0.5 * 252**0.5))
    assert annualize(vol, periods_per_year=4).values["A"] == (None, 1.0)

"""RISK-04: 252 x mean, sqrt(252) x sample stdev, their Sharpe (0 at zero vol), the last rolling vol (TC-07)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, VolSeries, summary_stats

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def vol_of(data: dict) -> VolSeries:
    r = data["returns"]
    return VolSeries(
        returns=Returns(
            base_date=r["base_date"],
            dates=tuple(r["dates"]),
            tickers=tuple(r["tickers"]),
            values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in r["values"].items()},
        ),
        window=data["window"],
        annualized=data["annualized"],
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in data["values"].items()},
    )


def test_tc07_whole_period_summary(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-07")
    summary = summary_stats(vol_of(given["vol"]))
    tol = expected["tol"]
    assert [s.ticker for s in summary] == [e["ticker"] for e in expected["summary"]]
    for got, want in zip(summary, expected["summary"], strict=True):
        assert got.ann_return == pytest.approx(want["ann_return"], abs=tol, rel=0)
        assert got.ann_vol == pytest.approx(want["ann_vol"], abs=tol, rel=0)
        assert got.sharpe == pytest.approx(want["sharpe"], abs=tol, rel=0)
        if want["last_vol"] is None:
            assert got.last_vol is None
        else:
            assert got.last_vol == pytest.approx(want["last_vol"], abs=tol, rel=0)

D314Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/summary.py (31 lines)

"""PL-03 step 2: each ticker's whole-period summary."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import statistics

from risk.types import TickerSummary, VolSeries


def summary_stats(vol: VolSeries, periods_per_year: int = 252) -> tuple[TickerSummary, ...]:
    """Annualized return, volatility, Sharpe and the last rolling vol per ticker.

    ValueError with fewer than two returns.
    """
    if len(vol.returns.dates) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"a summary needs at least two returns, got {len(vol.returns.dates)}")
    return tuple(_summary(vol, ticker, periods_per_year) for ticker in vol.returns.tickers)


def _summary(vol: VolSeries, ticker: str, periods_per_year: int) -> TickerSummary:
    values = vol.returns.values[ticker]
    ann_return = periods_per_year * statistics.mean(values)
    ann_vol = math.sqrt(periods_per_year) * statistics.stdev(values)
    return TickerSummary(
        ticker=ticker,
        ann_return=ann_return,
        ann_vol=ann_vol,
        sharpe=ann_return / ann_vol if ann_vol else 0.0,
        last_vol=next((v for v in reversed(vol.values[ticker]) if v is not None), None),
    )

"""RISK-05: the deepest fall below the running peak, with the peak's and the trough's dates (TC-08)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from risk import Returns, max_drawdown

pytestmark = pytest.mark.intent


def test_tc08_drawdown_with_dates(golden):
    given, expected = golden("risk", "TC-08")
    r = given["returns"]
    returns = Returns(
        base_date=r["base_date"],
        dates=tuple(r["dates"]),
        tickers=tuple(r["tickers"]),
        values={t: tuple(v) for t, v in r["values"].items()},
    )
    drawdowns = max_drawdown(returns)
    tol = expected["tol"]
    assert [d.ticker for d in drawdowns] == [e["ticker"] for e in expected["drawdowns"]]
    for got, want in zip(drawdowns, expected["drawdowns"], strict=True):
        assert got.depth == pytest.approx(want["depth"], abs=tol, rel=0)
        assert got.peak_date == want["peak_date"]
        assert got.trough_date == want["trough_date"]

D329Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/drawdown.py (28 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/packages/risk/src/risk/drawdown.py
"""PL-03 step 3: each ticker's maximum drawdown."""

from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
import math

from risk.types import Drawdown, Returns


def max_drawdown(returns: Returns) -> tuple[Drawdown, ...]:
    """The deepest fall below the running peak of wealth, which starts at 1 on ``base_date``."""
    return tuple(_drawdown(returns, ticker) for ticker in returns.tickers)


def _drawdown(returns: Returns, ticker: str) -> Drawdown:
    """trough / peak - 1 at its deepest; the first peak and trough when tied, both base_date if none."""
    dates = (returns.base_date, *returns.dates)
    wealth = [math.exp(s) for s in itertools.accumulate(returns.values[ticker], initial=0.0)]
    peak, peak_date = wealth[0], dates[0]
    worst = Drawdown(ticker=ticker, depth=0.0, peak_date=dates[0], trough_date=dates[0])
    for date, w in zip(dates, wealth, strict=True):
        if w > peak:
            peak, peak_date = w, date
        depth = w / peak - 1
        if depth < worst.depth:
            worst = Drawdown(ticker=ticker, depth=depth, peak_date=peak_date, trough_date=date)
    return worst

def test_dates_of_the_peak_and_the_trough():
    values = (math.log(1.2), math.log(0.9 / 1.2), math.log(1.5 / 0.9))
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": values})
    (d,) = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert (d.peak_date, d.trough_date) == ("d1", "d2")


def test_no_fall_dates_both_the_base_date():
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2"), ("A",), {"A": (0.01, 0.0)})
    (d,) = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert (d.depth, d.peak_date, d.trough_date) == (0.0, "d0", "d0")


def test_a_later_deeper_fall_from_a_new_peak_wins():
    # 1 -> 0.9 (-10% from d0), then 2.0 -> 1.6 (-20% from d2)
    values = (math.log(0.9), math.log(2.0 / 0.9), math.log(0.8))
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": values})
    (d,) = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert d.depth == pytest.approx(-0.2, abs=1e-12)
    assert (d.peak_date, d.trough_date) == ("d2", "d3")


def test_ties_keep_the_first():
    # two -50% falls from the same peak: d1 is the first trough
    values = (math.log(0.5), math.log(2.0), math.log(0.5))
    returns = Returns("d0", ("d1", "d2", "d3"), ("A",), {"A": values})
    (d,) = max_drawdown(returns)
    assert (d.peak_date, d.trough_date) == ("d0", "d1")

D340Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/minilab-A0-r3/with_skill/project/tests/system/test_e2e.py (67 lines)

"""PL-03 -> PL-02 -> PL-01 on a real CSV, no fakes: the real providers agree with their doubles."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

import mdata
import risk

CONTRACTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "contracts"
CSV = CONTRACTS / "mdata.load_prices" / "prices.csv"


def approx_rows(rows: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    return [
        {k: pytest.approx(v, abs=1e-12, rel=0) if isinstance(v, float) else v for k, v in row.items()}
        for row in rows
    ]


def test_real_load_prices_agrees_with_its_fake(contract_fake):
    fake = contract_fake("mdata.load_prices")
    assert mdata.load_prices(CSV) == fake.load_prices(CSV)


def test_real_vol_series_agrees_with_its_fake(contract_fake):
    fake = contract_fake("risk.vol_series").vol_series(CSV, window=2)
    real = risk.vol_series(CSV, window=2)
    assert real.window == fake.window
    assert real.annualized is fake.annualized is True
    assert real.returns.base_date == fake.returns.base_date
    assert real.returns.dates == fake.returns.dates
    for ticker in fake.returns.tickers:
        assert real.returns.values[ticker] == pytest.approx(fake.returns.values[ticker], abs=1e-12)
        assert real.values[ticker][:2] == fake.values[ticker][:2] == (None, None)
        assert real.values[ticker][2:] == pytest.approx(fake.values[ticker][2:], abs=1e-12)


def test_risk_report_end_to_end_matches_the_report_sample(contract_sample, tmp_path):
    out = tmp_path / "report.json"
    report = risk.risk_report(CSV, out, window=2)
    expected = contract_sample("risk.Report")["rows"]
    assert list(report.rows) == approx_rows(expected)
    assert json.loads(out.read_text()) == {"tickers": approx_rows(expected)}


def test_qr01_same_csv_same_window_same_file(tmp_path):
    a, b = tmp_path / "a.json", tmp_path / "b.json"
    risk.risk_report(CSV, a, window=2)
    risk.risk_report(CSV, b, window=2)
    assert a.read_bytes() == b.read_bytes()


def test_errors_reach_the_caller(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="ZZZ"):
        risk.risk_report(CSV, tmp_path / "r.json", window=2, tickers=["AAA", "ZZZ"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window"):
        risk.risk_report(CSV, tmp_path / "r.json", window=1)
    gappy = tmp_path / "gappy.csv"
    lines = CSV.read_text().splitlines()
    gappy.write_text("\n".join([*lines[:3], *(ln for ln in lines[3:] if ",BBB," not in ln)]) + "\n")
    with pytest.raises(mdata.GapError, match="BBB"):
        risk.risk_report(gappy, tmp_path / "r.json", window=2)
    assert not (tmp_path / "r.json").exists()

"""risk_report on a small real CSV: the window and tickers reach the steps, the file is the Report."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from risk import risk_report

CSV = """date,ticker,close
2024-03-01,AAA,100
2024-03-01,BBB,50
2024-03-04,AAA,110
2024-03-04,BBB,50
2024-03-05,AAA,99
2024-03-05,BBB,51
2024-03-06,AAA,104
2024-03-06,BBB,
2024-03-07,AAA,120
2024-03-07,BBB,52
"""


@pytest.fixture
def csv_path(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "prices.csv"
    path.write_text(CSV)
    return path


def test_one_sorted_row_per_ticker_in_the_file(csv_path, tmp_path):
    out = tmp_path / "out.json"
    report = risk_report(csv_path, out, window=2)
    assert [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows] == ["AAA", "BBB"]
    assert json.loads(out.read_text()) == {"tickers": [dict(row) for row in report.rows]}


def test_drawdown_dates_come_from_the_calendar(csv_path, tmp_path):
    aaa = risk_report(csv_path, tmp_path / "out.json", window=2).rows[0]
    assert (aaa["peak_date"], aaa["trough_date"]) == ("2024-03-04", "2024-03-05")
    assert aaa["max_drawdown"] == pytest.approx(99 / 110 - 1, abs=1e-12)


def test_the_window_reaches_rolling_vol(csv_path, tmp_path):
    # four returns: window 3 leaves one value, window 4 none
    assert risk_report(csv_path, tmp_path / "a.json", window=3).rows[0]["last_vol"] is not None
    assert risk_report(csv_path, tmp_path / "b.json", window=4).rows[0]["last_vol"] is None


def test_tickers_pick_the_rows(csv_path, tmp_path):
    report = risk_report(csv_path, tmp_path / "out.json", window=2, tickers=["BBB"])
    assert [row["ticker"] for row in report.rows] == ["BBB"]

full text
All 21 tasks of M1 are built and committed on `build/M1`. Each task's gate passed, and `pytest tests` runs 100 tests, all passing: the three smoke tests, TC-01 to TC-08, the contract tests and the system tests. But `scripts/check.sh full --exit` (the milestone exit check) **still fails two checks**. Both need a change to protected files, so they're your call.

**Exit-check failures:**
1. **vulture:** it reports `sharpe` and `last_vol` on `TickerSummary` (`packages/risk/src/risk/types.py:36-37`) as unused, at 60% confidence. They are used: `write_report` copies them into each row with `dataclasses.asdict`, which vulture can't follow. The fix is two lines in `scripts/vulture_whitelist.py`, which is protected and owned by build-setup. The other option is to have `report.py` name each field explicitly.
2. **typos:** it flags the commit hash `ba32044` in the `**Base:**` field of the `tasks/plan.md` header as a misspelling of "by". That line was already there before the build; the fix is an ignore rule in the typos config in `pyproject.toml`, also protected.

Everything else in the exit check passes, with 100% branch coverage and pip-audit finding no known vulnerabilities.

**What changed beyond the plan:**
- **Drawdown contract:** `risk.Drawdown` now has `trough_date`, from the revision you approved. It's in its own commit, before M1-T01.
- **Last rolling volatility:** TC-07 expects the last value that exists, since X's series ends without one, so `summary_stats` reports that rather than the final entry.
- **Gap rules:** a run of exactly 5 missing dates is filled. A gap at the end of a ticker's data counts toward the limit too.
- **Duplicates:** a later row that is unusable doesn't replace an earlier usable one for the same date and ticker.
- **Contract fixture:** `tests/contracts/mdata.load_prices/prices.csv` loads exactly to the Prices sample, and at window 2 it gives the VolSeries sample. That's how the system tests check that the real functions and their fakes agree.
- **Doc cleanup:** a final commit removes the "planned layout" sections from both `pipelines.md` files, now that every pipeline is built. `dt lint` passes with no errors.

**Gaps you should know about:**
- **System tests:** `scripts/check.sh` never runs `tests/system/`. Its test list covers only `tests/intent`, `tests/unit` and `tests/contracts`, so `tests/system/test_e2e.py` runs only under a plain `pytest tests`. The check script probably needs that directory added, which is another build-setup change.
- **No reviews:** I didn't run the review checklists at the four checkpoints or record any findings, because this run had no reviewer agent. That review is still to do.
- **Roadmap:** `dt status` says the roadmap's status column is stale (`dt roadmap`). I left it alone.

How do you want to handle the two exit failures: whitelist and typos ignore through build-setup, or a different fix? And should I merge `build/M1` into `main` now, or after those are fixed?
